独立评测发现

来源：独立评审 · 口径 v2 · 源 Run 37621548882 / attempt 1

最新上游状态：未复核。原评审“已解决”不作为上游修复证明；应用绕行、业务 QA 通过均不关闭框架问题。

等级与类型怎么定
  • 严重阻塞交付，或造成数据丢失、安全问题，且没有合理绕行
  • 重要按指引或公开 API 正常使用会静默得到错误结果，或只能用非公开手段绕行
  • 轻微能完成，但要多花排查成本或在应用侧绕行
  • 信息措辞、示例完整度等不影响结果的改进
  • 实现缺陷实现违背文档或公开约定
  • 能力缺口需求属于框架职责，但缺少能力或公开接入
  • 指引错误指引、示例或注释写错，或彼此矛盾，照做会出错
  • 指引缺失指引缺少必要说明或示例，需要读源码或试探
  • 易用性改进现有能力可用，但有改进空间

F5 · 文件内容字节路由不受 exposure Policy 影响，缺逐记录授权扩展点

NocoBase3 插件 · @nocobase/app-plugin-file · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-file/SKILL.md · 原评审：未解决

⌄

反馈证据核对

定向复核未完成：Pi invocation timed out after 180 seconds.

    反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

    app-plugin-file 的 accessPath/<uuid> 内容路由刻意绕过 exposure Policy，仅凭不可猜 UUID 服务任何人；框架只提供“应用自行在贡献路径上加认证/授权”的路径，未提供把内容路由绑定到父记录关系的逐记录授权原语。本项目因此关闭 read metadata 并在详情页自行构造 contentUrl，内容路由仅由会话守卫兜底。

    实际影响
    受限业务附件的内容访问难以在框架层表达逐记录授权，只能依赖会话 + UUID 保密，属于可被链接分享绕过的风险面。
    建议改法
    为内容路由提供逐记录授权扩展点（例如按集合/关系解析 principal 的 scope 后再回源），并把可行方案写入 file Skill。
    诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
    触发条件
    用 file exposure 的内容路由提供受业务规则约束的附件。
    应有能力 / 行为
    内容路由可绑定逐记录授权（如按父记录关系过滤）。
    实际观察
    字节路由不受 Policy 影响，框架未提供逐记录授权钩子；应用只能加会话守卫并以 UUID 兜底。
    应用绕行与代价
    在贡献路由上按 accessPath/* 前缀自挂应用认证并做记录级校验，或把内容代理进 /api 由业务路由鉴权后回源。
    改进后的验收标准
    存在受支持的逐记录内容授权扩展点或文档化的标准方案，并有覆盖跨用户访问的测试。
    评审时证据 · 冻结快照 4 条
    packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-file/dist/server/routes.d.ts:20–46E54

    app-plugin-file 路由类型：exposure 的 policy 为 RepositoryPolicy；注释明确 accessPath 下的公开字节路由是刻意例外，不受该 Policy 影响，仅凭 UUID 服务任何人。

        /**
     * The Policy every action of this exposure runs under, including uploads.
     *
     * `uploadOne` and `uploadMany` do not go through `defineRepositoryApiRoutes`
     * — they compose their own values and call the Repository directly — so what
     * they bind is derived from this one: the `create` scope and defaults are
     * inherited, and the field allowlist is replaced by the file columns, which
     * is the only thing a caller cannot influence here. See `uploadPolicy`.
     *
     * The public byte route under `accessPath` is the deliberate exception. It
     * serves anyone holding the UUID, has no authentication of its own, and is
     * unaffected by this Policy.
     */
    readonly policy: RepositoryPolicy | ((principal: P) => RepositoryPolicy);
    readonly actions: FileRepositoryApiActions;
}
export interface FileRepositoryRoutesApplication {
    readonly container: ServiceContainer;
    readonly publicBasePath?: string;
}
export interface DefineFileRepositoryApiRoutesOptions<P = unknown> {
    readonly repositories: readonly FileRepositoryApiExposure<P>[];
    /** Resolve the principal a Policy function receives. See `app-server`. */
    readonly principal?: (context: Context) => P | Promise<P>;
}
/** Public first-version routes. Authentication and authorization are application-owned. */
export declare function defineFileRepositoryApiRoutes<P = unknown>(options: DefineFileRepositoryApiRoutesOptions<P>): readonly AppRouteContribution<FileRepositoryRoutesApplication>[];
    packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-file/dist/server/repository.js:15–50E55

    app-plugin-file：uploadPolicy 将 create 的 scope/defaults 继承、字段替换为文件列，read 固定 scope:true 服务上传回执与公开字节路由；update/delete 恒为 false。

        'createdAt',
    'updatedAt',
];
/**
 * The Policy the upload path binds, derived from the exposure's own.
 *
 * It is derived rather than used as it stands because an upload supplies no
 * caller fields at all — `store()` composes every value — so a field allowlist
 * has nothing to constrain here and the plugin substitutes its own columns.
 * What is inherited is exactly the two dimensions that do not describe caller
 * input: which rows this principal may produce (`scope`), and what the server
 * stamps onto them (`defaults`). A `create` that is `false` stays `false`, so
 * an exposure that forbids creating rows forbids uploading them too.
 *
 * This is the only place a Policy is widened, and the reason is specific to
 * this path. It licenses nothing elsewhere.
 */
export function uploadPolicy(base) {
    const create = base.create === true || base.create === false
        ? base.create
        : {
            scope: base.create.scope,
            defaults: base.create.defaults,
            fields: FILE_COLUMNS,
            relations: {},
        };
    // `read` serves this path's own returning record and the public byte route,
    // both of which need every column. `update` and `delete` never run here.
    return {
        create,
        read: { scope: true, fields: FILE_COLUMNS, relations: {} },
        update: false,
        delete: false,
    };
}
export class FileRepositoryError extends Error {
    app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-file/SKILL.md:122–128E56

    file Skill：policy 治理 exposure 的所有动作；accessPath/<uuid> 内容路由在 /api 之外；这些是公开路由，需应用自挂持路径前缀的认证与授权，且不得 use('*')。

    `policy` is required and governs every action of the exposure, uploads included. It is a Repository Policy: `read`, `create`, `update` and `delete`, each `true`, `false`, or a rule node. A node that is `false` refuses that operation; a node with no `fields` accepts no caller-supplied field, which is what a metadata `createOne` needs before it will accept anything. Pass a function of the principal — `policy: (principal) => ({ ... })` — together with a `principal(context)` resolver to scope rows to the caller; without a principal the request is refused with 403.

An upload supplies no caller fields, so it binds a Policy derived from this one: the `create` scope and defaults are inherited and the field allowlist is replaced by the file columns. `create: false` therefore forbids uploading as well as creating metadata — decided, with the principal, before the body limit, the content type or the multipart body is read, so a refused upload answers `403 WRITE_FORBIDDEN` (domain `app`) and stores nothing — and a `create.defaults` of `{ ownerId }` is stamped onto uploaded rows — which is what keeps an uploaded file inside the same scope `findMany` reads. The content route under `accessPath` is the exception: it is public and this Policy does not reach it.

Content is GET `<accessPath>/<uuid>.<ext>` outside `/api`, omitting the dot when extensionless. Stream returns full bytes as an attachment; redirect returns a public storage URL or a five-minute signed URL. A disk without URL support requires stream mode; there is no automatic fallback.

These are public routes. For restricted files, register App-owned authentication and authorization on the paths each contribution owns before mounting it — `/<name>/<action>` per exposed API action, `<accessPath>/*` for content. Never `router.use('*', ...)` in a contribution router: contributions share the mounted router, so it also guards the SPA and every contribution mounted after yours. Check the operation and record/parent-record access; a login page, private disk, Client filter or Policy field allowlist is not authentication. Add scoped middleware and principal-based Policy to the generated routes first. If an existing contract needs an adapter, extend that module using the public Server manager and retain its authorization checks; missing built-in authentication alone is not a reason to replace the routes. Never trust browser-supplied ownership.
    app/server/routes/expense-files.ts:24–60E57

    本项目发票 exposure：read/update/delete 均 false，注释解释 File Plugin 的 Policy 无法表达“申请人/部门主管/财务可读”这种跨关系过滤；内容路由由应用按前缀加会话守卫兜底。

    const invoiceExposure = {
  name: EXPENSE_INVOICE_REPOSITORY,
  collection: EXPENSE_INVOICE_REPOSITORY,
  disk: 'local',
  accessPath: EXPENSE_INVOICE_ACCESS_PATH,
  accessMode: 'stream' as const,
  /*
   * Upload only.
   *
   * `read`, `update` and `delete` are false because the File Plugin's Policy cannot express this application's rule:
   * an invoice may be read by the applicant, the manager of the claim's department and finance, which means a filter
   * over a relation the Plugin has no way to write (`expenseItems.invoiceId` -> `expenseClaims`). Leaving `read` open
   * would list and serve every invoice in the application to every signed-in user, so it is closed instead, and the
   * detail page shows an invoice by building its content URL from the ids it already has.
   *
   * `create.scope: true` because an upload supplies no caller fields at all: the Plugin composes `disk`, `key`,
   * `filename`, `ext`, `mimeType` and `size` itself, so there is nothing for a field allowlist to constrain.
   */
  policy: {
    read: false as const,
    create: { scope: true as const },
    update: false as const,
    delete: false as const,
  },
  actions: {
    uploadOne: { maxSize: MAX_INVOICE_SIZE },
    uploadMany: { maxSize: MAX_INVOICE_SIZE },
  },
};

/**
 * The Plugin generates its routes without authentication: "Authentication and authorization are application-owned".
 * The content route in particular is otherwise public, keyed only by an unguessable UUID. Every path the exposure owns
 * is therefore wrapped in a session guard here, scoped to those exact prefixes — a contribution shares its mounted
 * router with every other contribution, so an unscoped `use('*')` would guard routes this module does not own.
 */
function guardedPaths(prefixes: readonly string[]): string[] {
    上游 Issue 草稿 · Markdown
    ### [轻微][能力缺口] 文件内容字节路由不受 exposure Policy 影响，缺逐记录授权扩展点

**相关对象**：`@nocobase/app-plugin-file`（评审时 1.0.0-beta.18）、`app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-file/SKILL.md`

**反馈证据核对**：候选反馈：证据不足

定向复核未完成：Pi invocation timed out after 180 seconds.


反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

app-plugin-file 的 accessPath/<uuid> 内容路由刻意绕过 exposure Policy，仅凭不可猜 UUID 服务任何人；框架只提供“应用自行在贡献路径上加认证/授权”的路径，未提供把内容路由绑定到父记录关系的逐记录授权原语。本项目因此关闭 read metadata 并在详情页自行构造 contentUrl，内容路由仅由会话守卫兜底。

**影响**：受限业务附件的内容访问难以在框架层表达逐记录授权，只能依赖会话 + UUID 保密，属于可被链接分享绕过的风险面。

**建议改动**：为内容路由提供逐记录授权扩展点（例如按集合/关系解析 principal 的 scope 后再回源），并把可行方案写入 file Skill。

**验收标准**：存在受支持的逐记录内容授权扩展点或文档化的标准方案，并有覆盖跨用户访问的测试。

- `app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-file/SKILL.md:122–128` — file Skill：policy 治理 exposure 的所有动作；accessPath/<uuid> 内容路由在 /api 之外；这些是公开路由，需应用自挂持路径前缀的认证与授权，且不得 use('*')。

    F1 · 种子共享 seed-data 模块的推荐写法在进程内测试路径下解析失败

    文档 / Skill / 示例 · @nocobase/db · 原评审：未解决

    ⌄

    反馈证据核对

    定向复核未完成：Pi invocation timed out after 180 seconds.

      反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

      nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/references/code-and-seeds.md 建议种子 `import { engineer } from '../../seed-data/permission-sets.js'`。但 seed 由 @nocobase/db 的 importTaskDefinition 加载：CLI 下走 tsx 可把 .js 解析到 .ts，进程内测试则由 Node 原生类型剥离直接加载，相对 ./x.js 不会解析到 x.ts。该函数只对入口文件调用 __rewriteRelativeImportExtension，不处理种子内部的相对导入。

      实际影响
      按指引编写的种子会在“真实测试库”路径下报 Cannot find module，作者被迫内联共享常量，削弱复用并增加重复。
      建议改法
      把示例改为跨路径可加载的写法（无扩展名或显式 .ts 说明），或在 @nocobase/db 的任务加载器中对传递依赖同样做 TS 扩展名解析。
      诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
      触发条件
      按 code-and-seeds.md 示例在 seed 中导入 ../../seed-data/*.js。
      应有能力 / 行为
      同一种子在 CLI、dev server 与进程内测试三条路径下都能加载。
      实际观察
      进程内测试路径下报 Cannot find module '.../seed-data/expense-permission-sets.js'；db 仅改写入口文件扩展名。
      应用绕行与代价
      把共享声明内联进单个种子（本项目已在 202610010011_expense_departments.ts 中内联并注释原因）。
      改进后的验收标准
      示例更新为可跨路径加载，或加载器对传递依赖执行同等解析，且新增回归测试覆盖进程内测试路径。
      评审时证据 · 冻结快照 5 条
      app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/references/code-and-seeds.md:55–100E43

      code-and-seeds.md 的种子示例：defineSeed 中 `import { engineer } from '../../seed-data/permission-sets.js';`，并说明该种子位于 database/main/seeds/*.ts、导入 database/seed-data/permission-sets.ts。

        .build();
```

The declarations used here are in [the sales workflow](business-module.md). Register `sales.prepared` and `sales.region` before serving requests. A page grant can equally be written as `authz.pages.grant('sales.quotes')` where the service is available; a seed has no service, so it writes the plain grant. Use `{ key, ns }` titles and matching client translations in a localized product; plain titles here keep the snippets small. Grants store record access keys and parameters, never resolver functions. Each business data scope value is a record access key or a record selection; `''` selects nothing.

## A normal App seed has a database context

`defineSeed` from `@nocobase/db` receives `{ query, connection }`. `connection` is a restricted `SeedConnection`, not a complete runtime `DatabaseConnection`; the seed has no application container. Do not cast it to construct an App authorization service or assume `authorizationToken` can be resolved there.

For initial installation, a seed may write the documented persistence rows below after the owning plugins' migrations. Keep this adapter confined to installation data, use fluent values as its input, and test against the installed schema. Runtime routes must use the services instead. Keep one seed-data module per table.

```ts
import { defineSeed } from '@nocobase/db';
import { encodeAuthorizationTitle } from '@nocobase/authorization/core';
import { engineer } from '../../seed-data/permission-sets.js';

export default defineSeed({
  name: '202609190001_sales_jobs',
  transaction: true,
  async run({ query }) {
    const existing = await query
      .selectFrom('authorizationPermissionSets')
      .select('id')
      .where('key', '=', engineer.key)
      .executeTakeFirst();
    if (existing) return; // Preserve an administrator's existing definition.
    const now = new Date();
    await query
      .insertInto('authorizationPermissionSets')
      .values({
        id: engineer.key,
        key: engineer.key,
        title: encodeAuthorizationTitle(engineer.title),
        grants: JSON.stringify(engineer.grants),
        createdAt: now,
        updatedAt: now,
      })
      .execute();
  },
});
```

Ensure schema migrations for authorization and business collections have run, and explicitly order prerequisite installation data. Use the App's existing migration/seed commands. ### Persist initial permission-set assignments
      app/database/main/seeds/202610010011_expense_departments.ts:29–38E44

      本项目种子注释：该声明故意内联在 seed 内而非共享 database/seed-data/ 模块——seed 由 importTaskDefinition 加载，CLI 下走 tsx，但进程内测试由 Node 直接加载，相对 ./x.js 说明符在第二条路径不会解析到 x.ts。

       * The declaration lives in this seed rather than a shared `database/seed-data/` module on purpose. A seed is loaded as
 * raw TypeScript by `importTaskDefinition()` — under `tsx` from the CLI, but directly by Node in an in-process test —
 * and a relative `./x.js` specifier does not resolve to `x.ts` on that second path. A seed that imports nothing of its
 * own loads the same way in the CLI, the dev server and a test.
 */
const expenseMember = definePermissionSet('expense-member')
  .title('Expense member')
  .grant({
    resource: { type: 'page', id: EXPENSE_CLAIMS_PAGE_ID },
    actions: [{ action: 'access' }],
      packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/migration/internal/task-loader.js:1–12E39

      task-loader 的 __rewriteRelativeImportExtension 只对传入的入口文件路径做 .ts/.tsx 扩展名改写，DEFAULT_TASK_EXTENSIONS 含 .ts。

      var __rewriteRelativeImportExtension = (this && this.__rewriteRelativeImportExtension) || function (path, preserveJsx) {
    if (typeof path === "string" && /^\.\.?\//.test(path)) {
        return path.replace(/\.(tsx)$|((?:\.d)?)((?:\.[^./]+?)?)\.([cm]?)ts$/i, function (m, tsx, d, ext, cm) {
            return tsx ? preserveJsx ? ".jsx" : ".js" : d && (!ext || !cm) ? m : (d + ext + "." + cm.toLowerCase() + "js");
        });
    }
    return path;
};
import { readdir } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { basename, extname } from 'node:path';
import { pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';
export const DEFAULT_TASK_EXTENSIONS = ['.js', '.mjs', '.cjs', '.ts'];
      packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/migration/internal/task-loader.js:50–62E40

      importTaskDefinition 仅对入口 URL 调用 __rewriteRelativeImportExtension 后 import；其内部相对导入的解析不由该函数处理。

          }
    return extensions.has(extname(fileName));
}
export function taskNameFromFileName(fileName) {
    return basename(fileName, extname(fileName));
}
/** The mtime query keeps a re-read after an edit from serving a cached module. */
export async function importTaskDefinition(filePath, mtimeMs) {
    const url = pathToFileURL(filePath);
    url.searchParams.set('mtime', String(Math.trunc(mtimeMs)));
    const module = await import(__rewriteRelativeImportExtension(url.href));
    return module.default;
}

      上游 Issue 草稿 · Markdown
      ### [轻微][指引缺失] 种子共享 seed-data 模块的推荐写法在进程内测试路径下解析失败

**相关对象**：`@nocobase/db`（评审时 1.0.0-beta.17）

**反馈证据核对**：候选反馈：证据不足

定向复核未完成：Pi invocation timed out after 180 seconds.


反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/references/code-and-seeds.md 建议种子 `import { engineer } from '../../seed-data/permission-sets.js'`。但 seed 由 @nocobase/db 的 importTaskDefinition 加载：CLI 下走 tsx 可把 .js 解析到 .ts，进程内测试则由 Node 原生类型剥离直接加载，相对 ./x.js 不会解析到 x.ts。该函数只对入口文件调用 __rewriteRelativeImportExtension，不处理种子内部的相对导入。

**影响**：按指引编写的种子会在“真实测试库”路径下报 Cannot find module，作者被迫内联共享常量，削弱复用并增加重复。

**建议改动**：把示例改为跨路径可加载的写法（无扩展名或显式 .ts 说明），或在 @nocobase/db 的任务加载器中对传递依赖同样做 TS 扩展名解析。

**验收标准**：示例更新为可跨路径加载，或加载器对传递依赖执行同等解析，且新增回归测试覆盖进程内测试路径。

**评审时证据（冻结快照）**：
- `app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/references/code-and-seeds.md:55–100` — code-and-seeds.md 的种子示例：defineSeed 中 `import { engineer } from '../../seed-data/permission-sets.js';`，并说明该种子位于 database/main/seeds/*.ts、导入 database/seed-data/permission-sets.ts。
- `app/database/main/seeds/202610010011_expense_departments.ts:29–38` — 本项目种子注释：该声明故意内联在 seed 内而非共享 database/seed-data/ 模块——seed 由 importTaskDefinition 加载，CLI 下走 tsx，但进程内测试由 Node 直接加载，相对 ./x.js 说明符在第二条路径不会解析到 x.ts。
- `packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/migration/internal/task-loader.js:1–12` — task-loader 的 __rewriteRelativeImportExtension 只对传入的入口文件路径做 .ts/.tsx 扩展名改写，DEFAULT_TASK_EXTENSIONS 含 .ts。
- `packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/migration/internal/task-loader.js:50–62` — importTaskDefinition 仅对入口 URL 调用 __rewriteRelativeImportExtension 后 import；其内部相对导入的解析不由该函数处理。
- `artifacts/agent-history/agent-implement.jsonl/part-0050.txt:1855–1862` — 搭建过程日志：种子相对导入在测试路径下失败，出现 Cannot find module '.../database/seed-data/expense-permission-sets.js'（该信号未进入 index 的 68 条跟踪错误，但已被应用作者在种子里显式规避）。

      F4 · testing.md 示例使用裸 expect，与模板 vitest 配置（未开 globals）不一致

      文档 / Skill / 示例 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/testing.md · 原评审：未解决

      ⌄

      反馈证据核对

      定向复核未完成：Pi invocation timed out after 180 seconds.

        反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

        testing.md 的整应用测试示例直接使用裸 expect(...)，而 app/vitest.config.ts 未设置 globals:true。测试运行时报 ReferenceError: expect is not defined。

        实际影响
        照抄示例会立即失败，Agent 需自行推断应显式导入 expect。
        建议改法
        示例统一 `import { expect } from 'vitest'`，或在模板 vitest 配置开启 globals 并在指引中说明。
        诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
        触发条件
        按 testing.md 示例写测试并使用裸 expect。
        应有能力 / 行为
        示例在模板默认 vitest 配置下即可运行。
        实际观察
        模板未开启 globals，示例未导入 expect，报 ReferenceError: expect is not defined。
        应用绕行与代价
        测试文件显式 import { expect } from 'vitest'。
        改进后的验收标准
        示例显式导入 expect（或模板开启 globals），并在指引中保持一致。

        testing.md 的整应用测试示例：createAppTest({ createServer: createStandaloneServer })，使用裸 expect(...) 且未展示 config.auth.secret，只演示了一个 GET。

        ## Testing through the whole application

To check a route's authentication and permission boundaries as a user meets them, start the application itself on test databases with `@nocobase/app-testing/server` and sign in through its own sign-in route with `@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/testing`:

```ts
import {
  DEFAULT_ADMIN_CREDENTIALS,
  signIn,
} from '@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/testing';
import { createAppTest } from '@nocobase/app-testing/server';
import { createStandaloneServer } from '../../server/standalone.ts';

const test = createAppTest({ createServer: createStandaloneServer });

test('lists orders for an administrator only', async ({ testApp, request }) => {
  expect((await request('/orders')).status).toBe(401);
  const admin = await signIn(testApp, DEFAULT_ADMIN_CREDENTIALS);
  expect((await admin.fetch('/orders')).status).toBe(200);
});
        app/vitest.config.ts:18–32E60

        模板 vitest 配置：include 为 tests/**/*.test.{ts,tsx}，setupFiles 只引入 client-config，未开启 globals。

            server: {
      deps: {
        // Transform the public Client surfaces so vi.mock applies, without duplicating the Server packages' identity tokens.
        inline: [
          /@nocobase\/app-plugin-authorization\/(?:dist\/)?client\//u,
          /@nocobase\/app-plugin-notification-in-app\/(?:dist\/)?client\//u,
        ],
      },
    },
    // A glob rather than a list of filenames. The list had to be edited by hand for every test added or removed and
    // silently drifted: it named a file that no longer existed while several real test files were absent from it, so
    // those tests were never run at all.
    include: ['tests/**/*.test.{ts,tsx}'],
    setupFiles: ['./tests/setup/client-config.ts'],
    // Keep the command usable if an application intentionally removes all scaffold tests before adding its own.

        ### [轻微][指引缺失] testing.md 示例使用裸 expect，与模板 vitest 配置（未开 globals）不一致

**相关对象**：`app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/testing.md`

**反馈证据核对**：候选反馈：证据不足

定向复核未完成：Pi invocation timed out after 180 seconds.


反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

testing.md 的整应用测试示例直接使用裸 expect(...)，而 app/vitest.config.ts 未设置 globals:true。测试运行时报 ReferenceError: expect is not defined。

**影响**：照抄示例会立即失败，Agent 需自行推断应显式导入 expect。

**建议改动**：示例统一 `import { expect } from 'vitest'`，或在模板 vitest 配置开启 globals 并在指引中说明。

**验收标准**：示例显式导入 expect（或模板开启 globals），并在指引中保持一致。

**评审时证据（冻结快照）**：
- `app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/testing.md:52–70` — testing.md 的整应用测试示例：createAppTest({ createServer: createStandaloneServer })，使用裸 expect(...) 且未展示 config.auth.secret，只演示了一个 GET。
- `app/vitest.config.ts:18–32` — 模板 vitest 配置：include 为 tests/**/*.test.{ts,tsx}，setupFiles 只引入 client-config，未开启 globals。
- `artifacts/agent-history/agent-implement.jsonl/part-0077.txt:715–719` — 搭建过程的 vitest 输出：expense-api.test.ts 因裸 expect 报 ReferenceError: expect is not defined；同一文件中 createClaim 请求以 403 失败，响应 reason 为 INVALID_CSRF_ORIGIN，直接印证测试助手缺 Origin 与 vitest 未开 globals 两处衔接缺口。

          实际观察

          应用绕行与代价
          在 createAppTest({ config: { auth: { secret: '...' } } }) 中显式提供。
          改进后的验收标准
          测试库自动注入测试 secret，或文档示例明确展示该配置并通过一条整应用测试。

          function testAppConfigValues(databases, options) {
    const extra = options.config ?? {};
    const extraDatabase = recordOf(extra.database);
    const extraConnections = recordOf(extraDatabase.connections);
    const install = options.install !== false;
    const connections = Object.fromEntries(databases.connections.map((name) => [
        name,
        {
            ...serializableConnection(databases.connectionConfig(name)),
            // Spelled out both ways: an application runs its migrations at startup unless told otherwise.
            migrations: { autoRun: install },
            seeds: { autoRun: install },
            ...recordOf(extraConnections[name]),
        },
    ]));
    return {
        ...extra,
        database: {
            ...extraDatabase,
            connections: { ...extraConnections, ...connections },
        },
    };
}

          packages/@nocobase/app-testing/dist/src/server/app.js:9–50E59

          createTestApp 初始化服务、数据库与 request 前缀，返回 server/application/database/request/close，但未设置 auth.secret 或可信来源。

          export async function createTestApp(options) {
    const { createServer, server: serverOptions = {}, ...configOptions } = options;
    const config = await createTestAppConfig(configOptions);
    let server;
    try {
        server = await createServer({
            viteDevUrl: false,
            ...serverOptions,
            configPath: config.path,
            env: {
                APP_STORAGE_DIR: path.join(config.directory, 'storage'),
                ...serverOptions.env,
            },
        });
    }
    catch (error) {
        await config.dispose();
        throw error;
    }
    const { application } = server;
    const database = application.container.resolve(databaseManagerToken);
    const apiRoot = `http://localhost${trimTrailingSlash(application.publicBasePath)}/api`;
    const fetch = (request) => server.fetch(request);
    return {
        server,
        application,
        publicBasePath: application.publicBasePath,
        fetch,
        database,
        connection: database.connection(),
        config,
        request: async (target, init) => fetch(new Request(target.startsWith('/') ? `${apiRoot}${target}` : target, init)),
        close: async () => {
            try {
                await server.close();
            }
            finally {
                await config.dispose();
            }
        },
    };
}

          ## Testing through the whole application

To check a route's authentication and permission boundaries as a user meets them, start the application itself on test databases with `@nocobase/app-testing/server` and sign in through its own sign-in route with `@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/testing`:

```ts
import {
  DEFAULT_ADMIN_CREDENTIALS,
  signIn,
} from '@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/testing';
import { createAppTest } from '@nocobase/app-testing/server';
import { createStandaloneServer } from '../../server/standalone.ts';

const test = createAppTest({ createServer: createStandaloneServer });

test('lists orders for an administrator only', async ({ testApp, request }) => {
  expect((await request('/orders')).status).toBe(401);
  const admin = await signIn(testApp, DEFAULT_ADMIN_CREDENTIALS);
  expect((await admin.fetch('/orders')).status).toBe(200);
});

          ### [轻微][易用性改进] createAppTest 不提供 auth.secret，testing.md 示例照抄即启动失败

**相关对象**：`app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/testing.md`、`@nocobase/app-testing`（评审时 0.1.0-beta.1）

**反馈证据核对**：候选反馈：证据不足

定向复核未完成：Pi invocation timed out after 180 seconds.


反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

@nocobase/app-testing 的 createTestAppConfig 只把调用方 config 合并进数据库连接，createTestApp 也未设置 auth.secret；而 app-plugin-authentication 启动时要求 config.secret。testing.md 的整应用示例只传 createServer，不展示 secret，照抄会报应用未配置。

          F3 · 测试登录助手缺 Origin，cookie 写请求被 CSRF 校验拒绝

          NocoBase3 插件 · @nocobase/app-plugin-authentication · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/testing.md · 原评审：未解决

          ⌄

          反馈证据核对

          定向复核未完成：Pi invocation timed out after 180 seconds.

            反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

            app-plugin-authentication/testing.js 的 signIn()/TestSession 返回的 fetch 会带上 cookie，但不设置 Origin 头；而同插件的 checkCookieWriteOrigin 要求携带 cookie 的非只读请求必须带可信 Origin/Referer，否则返回 403 INVALID_CSRF_ORIGIN。已在测试输出中观察到 create 请求以 403 失败。

            实际影响
            用测试助手调写接口的测试会在 CSRF 层失败，掩盖真正的业务断言，并消耗 Agent 排查时间。
            建议改法
            TestSession.fetch 默认发送 TEST_ORIGIN（或在类型/文档中明确要求调用方设置），使测试会话与浏览器同源写请求一致。
            诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
            触发条件
            用 signIn() 得到的会话 fetch 发起 POST/PUT/PATCH/DELETE。
            应有能力 / 行为
            测试会话的写请求与浏览器一致，通过 CSRF 校验。
            实际观察
            写请求无 Origin，被 checkCookieWriteOrigin 判为不可信来源并返回 403 INVALID_CSRF_ORIGIN。
            应用绕行与代价
            在测试请求头中手动加 Origin: http://localhost。
            改进后的验收标准
            测试助手默认发送可信 Origin，并有覆盖 cookie 写请求的回归测试。
            评审时证据 · 冻结快照 4 条
            packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/server/testing.js:19–50E47

            测试助手 signIn()/TestSession：DEFAULT_ADMIN_CREDENTIALS 与 TEST_ORIGIN，但返回的 fetch 只带 cookie/头，不设置 Origin，导致后续 cookie 写请求被 CSRF 校验拒绝。

            export async function signIn(app, credentials) {
    const apiRoot = `${TEST_ORIGIN}${trimTrailingSlash(app.publicBasePath)}/api`;
    const byUsername = 'username' in credentials;
    const response = await app.fetch(new Request(`${apiRoot}/auth/sign-in/${byUsername ? 'username' : 'email'}`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify(credentials),
    }));
    if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(`Sign-in as "${byUsername ? credentials.username : credentials.email}" failed with ${response.status}: ${await response.text()}`);
    }
    const body = (await response.json());
    if (!body.user) {
        throw new Error('Sign-in succeeded without returning the signed-in user.');
    }
    const cookie = response.headers
        .getSetCookie()
        .map((header) => header.split(';')[0])
        .join('; ');
    return {
        user: body.user,
        cookie,
        fetch: async (path, init = {}) => {
            const headers = new Headers(init.headers);
            headers.set('cookie', [headers.get('cookie'), cookie].filter(Boolean).join('; '));
            return app.fetch(new Request(path.startsWith('/') ? `${apiRoot}${path}` : path, {
                ...init,
                headers,
            }));
        },
    };
}
            packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/server/auth.js:125–162E46

            app-plugin-authentication 的 checkCookieWriteOrigin：携带 cookie 的非 GET/HEAD/OPTIONS 请求必须带可信 Origin/Referer，否则拒绝（INVALID_CSRF_ORIGIN）。

                /** Protect writes authenticated by a browser cookie, including routes that skip normal session lookup. */
    async checkCookieWriteOrigin(context) {
        if (['GET', 'HEAD', 'OPTIONS'].includes(context.req.method))
            return;
        // A credential header is not proof that it was used: an invalid API key may fall back to a valid cookie.
        // Cookie-free API key requests have no ambient browser credential to forge.
        if (!context.req.header('cookie'))
            return;
        const authContext = await this.auth.$context;
        const origin = context.req.header('origin');
        const inferredBaseURL = origin === 'null' &&
            context.req.header('sec-fetch-site') === 'same-origin'
            ? getBaseURL(undefined, authContext.options.basePath, context.req.raw, false, authContext.options.advanced?.trustedProxyHeaders)
            : undefined;
        const source = (inferredBaseURL ? getOrigin(inferredBaseURL) : undefined) ??
            origin ??
            context.req.header('referer');
        if (!source || source === 'null') {
            return invalidCsrfOrigin(context);
        }
        // Better Auth also accepts per-request trusted origins. Its static context contains the
        // configured base URL, static origins, plugin origins, and BETTER_AUTH_TRUSTED_ORIGINS.
        const configuredOrigins = authContext.options.trustedOrigins;
        const mergedOrigins = typeof configuredOrigins === 'function'
            ? await configuredOrigins(context.req.raw)
            : (configuredOrigins ?? []);
        const requestAuthContext = Object.create(authContext);
        requestAuthContext.trustedOrigins = [
            ...authContext.trustedOrigins,
            ...mergedOrigins.filter((origin) => typeof origin === 'string' && Boolean(origin)),
        ];
        const trustedByAuth = requestAuthContext.isTrustedOrigin(source, {
            allowRelativePaths: false,
        });
        if (!trustedByAuth) {
            return invalidCsrfOrigin(context);
        }
    }
            app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/testing.md:52–70E48

            testing.md 的整应用测试示例：createAppTest({ createServer: createStandaloneServer })，使用裸 expect(...) 且未展示 config.auth.secret，只演示了一个 GET。

            ## Testing through the whole application

To check a route's authentication and permission boundaries as a user meets them, start the application itself on test databases with `@nocobase/app-testing/server` and sign in through its own sign-in route with `@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/testing`:

```ts
import {
  DEFAULT_ADMIN_CREDENTIALS,
  signIn,
} from '@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/testing';
import { createAppTest } from '@nocobase/app-testing/server';
import { createStandaloneServer } from '../../server/standalone.ts';

const test = createAppTest({ createServer: createStandaloneServer });

test('lists orders for an administrator only', async ({ testApp, request }) => {
  expect((await request('/orders')).status).toBe(401);
  const admin = await signIn(testApp, DEFAULT_ADMIN_CREDENTIALS);
  expect((await admin.fetch('/orders')).status).toBe(200);
});

            上游 Issue 草稿 · Markdown
            ### [轻微][易用性改进] 测试登录助手缺 Origin，cookie 写请求被 CSRF 校验拒绝

**相关对象**：`@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication`（评审时 2.0.0-beta.0）、`app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/testing.md`

**反馈证据核对**：候选反馈：证据不足

定向复核未完成：Pi invocation timed out after 180 seconds.


反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

app-plugin-authentication/testing.js 的 signIn()/TestSession 返回的 fetch 会带上 cookie，但不设置 Origin 头；而同插件的 checkCookieWriteOrigin 要求携带 cookie 的非只读请求必须带可信 Origin/Referer，否则返回 403 INVALID_CSRF_ORIGIN。已在测试输出中观察到 create 请求以 403 失败。

**影响**：用测试助手调写接口的测试会在 CSRF 层失败，掩盖真正的业务断言，并消耗 Agent 排查时间。

**建议改动**：TestSession.fetch 默认发送 TEST_ORIGIN（或在类型/文档中明确要求调用方设置），使测试会话与浏览器同源写请求一致。

**验收标准**：测试助手默认发送可信 Origin，并有覆盖 cookie 写请求的回归测试。

**评审时证据（冻结快照）**：
- `packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/server/testing.js:19–50` — 测试助手 signIn()/TestSession：DEFAULT_ADMIN_CREDENTIALS 与 TEST_ORIGIN，但返回的 fetch 只带 cookie/头，不设置 Origin，导致后续 cookie 写请求被 CSRF 校验拒绝。
- `packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/server/auth.js:125–162` — app-plugin-authentication 的 checkCookieWriteOrigin：携带 cookie 的非 GET/HEAD/OPTIONS 请求必须带可信 Origin/Referer，否则拒绝（INVALID_CSRF_ORIGIN）。
- `app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/testing.md:52–70` — testing.md 的整应用测试示例：createAppTest({ createServer: createStandaloneServer })，使用裸 expect(...) 且未展示 config.auth.secret，只演示了一个 GET。
- `artifacts/agent-history/agent-implement.jsonl/part-0077.txt:715–719` — 搭建过程的 vitest 输出：expense-api.test.ts 因裸 expect 报 ReferenceError: expect is not defined；同一文件中 createClaim 请求以 403 失败，响应 reason 为 INVALID_CSRF_ORIGIN，直接印证测试助手缺 Origin 与 vitest 未开 globals 两处衔接缺口。

> 来源：nb3-factory 独立搭建评审（口径 v2，源 Run 37621548882 / attempt 1）。尚未复核最新上游源码。
            实现者过程与运行背景 · 8 个过程问题 / 8 条建议 · 不自动归为框架问题

            本任务已处理只描述应用侧处理结果，不代表 NocoBase3 上游已修复。

            本轮过程记录

            Built the whole expense reimbursement feature (schema, service, API, pages, seeds, tests) from scratch; the business code itself went smoothly and most of the time went into making the in-process test harness boot — seed loading, CSRF origin, and the public base path — and into the final lint sweep. Repair round 1 fixed the frozen-lockfile drift caused by moving the shadcn-added client packages to devDependencies in package.json without regenerating pnpm-lock.yaml.

            问题 01 · 实现阶段

            A source seed that imports another source .ts file through a .js specifier fails on the in-process test path

            处理结果未提供

            Running the app through `createAppTest` (install: true) failed to load the seed graph; the shared `./expense-permission-sets.js` specifier did not resolve to its `.ts` source. The same graph loaded fine under `tsx` from the CLI.

            原因（实现者自述）
            `importTaskDefinition()` imports the seed file with a `?mtime` URL and rewrites only the entry file's own relative `.js` extension. A relative `./x.js` specifier from a seed is not rewritten, and the vitest in-process app-test path imports the raw TypeScript with plain Node native type stripping, where `./x.js` is a literal, missing file.
            处理方式
            Made every expense seed self-contained: the permission-set declaration was inlined into `202610010011_expense_departments.ts`, the `database/seed-data/expense-permission-sets.ts` module was deleted, and a comment in the seed explains why.
            影响 / 代价：One refactor of the seeds plus one test round (~20 min); the constraint was only discovered when the API test first installed the app.来源：Agent · retro.json
            问题 02 · 实现阶段

            Cookie-authenticated writes in the app-test harness are rejected as INVALID_CSRF_ORIGIN

            处理结果未提供

            POST/PATCH requests sent by `TestSession.fetch` were answered `403` with reason `INVALID_CSRF_ORIGIN`, even though the session cookie was present.

            原因（实现者自述）
            `checkCookieWriteOrigin` resolves an origin from `Origin`/`Referer` and rejects a cookie write whose origin is missing or not trusted. `TestSession.fetch` adds no `Origin`, and `createAppTest` config had no `auth.trustedOrigins`. Adding only the request header was not enough; the origin must also be in the trusted list.
            处理方式
            The API test passes `config: { app: { publicOrigin: TEST_ORIGIN }, auth: { trustedOrigins: [TEST_ORIGIN] } }` and every write goes through a `write()` helper that sets `content-type: application/json` and `origin: TEST_ORIGIN`.
            影响 / 代价：About 3 debug rounds and ~15 minutes before the two-part requirement (header plus trusted list) was clear.来源：Agent · retro.json
            问题 03 · 实现阶段

            Manually built request URLs must include the application public base path

            处理结果未提供

            `fetch(new Request('http://localhost/uploads/...'))` returned a bare `404 Not found`; `/api/...` requests through `testApp.request()` worked.

            原因（实现者自述）
            The standalone server wraps the application in `createPublicBasePathAdapter`, which answers any pathname that does not start with `app.publicBasePath` (i.e. `/main`) with a 404. `testApp.request()` already prefixes `publicBasePath`, but a root-level file URL built by hand does not.
            处理方式
            Root-level URLs are built as `` `http://localhost${testApp.publicBasePath}/uploads/expenseInvoices/...` ``; the test destructures `testApp` instead of reading a `publicBasePath` viTest fixture (which does not exist and yields `undefined`).
            影响 / 代价：One debug round (~10 min); the 404 looked like a routing bug rather than a mounting detail.来源：Agent · retro.json
            问题 04 · 实现阶段

            The file plugin's content route is public by design, so private invoices would be readable by anyone with a UUID

            处理结果未提供

            The route `defineFileRepositoryApiRoutes` generates for `${accessPath}/:file` served file content without requiring a session.

            原因（实现者自述）
            The file plugin documents the content route as UUID-addressed and intentionally unauthenticated; it is meant to be embedded in contexts where the UUID is the capability.
            处理方式
            The route contribution wraps only the owned `${accessPath}/*` prefix with an app-owned `auth.required()` middleware, and `server/AGENTS.md` records that claim-level byte authorization beyond possession of the UUID is a deliberate limitation. Per-claim authorization for the bytes remains a documented gap.
            影响 / 代价：One design detour (~15 min); the private-invoice requirement would otherwise have been silently violated.来源：Agent · retro.json
            问题 05 · 自动验证

            ExpenseProvider.start() broke embedded hosts that mount the runtime without this app's migrations

            处理结果未提供

            Two `tests/logic/app-server.test.ts` failures: `RepositoryError: Collection "expenseExportJobs" does not exist.` in embedded test scopes that create a temp app root with no `database/` migrations.

            原因（实现者自述）
            Startup resume called `listUnfinishedExportJobs()` unconditionally; an embedded host can legitimately mount the runtime before/without the application schema.
            处理方式
            `listUnfinishedExportJobs()` returns `[]` when the `expenseExportJobs` collection is absent, so the provider boots on a schema-less runtime.
            影响 / 代价：One test round (~10 min).来源：Agent · retro.json
            问题 06 · 自动验证

            createAppTest requires an explicit auth secret, which the app never ships with a default

            处理结果未提供

            `ApplicationNotConfiguredError: auth.secret is not set.` when starting the app-test server.

            原因（实现者自述）
            `resolveAuthSecret` throws when no secret is configured, by design; `createAppTest` does not synthesise one.
            处理方式
            The expense API test passes a test-only `auth.secret`. Recorded so the next feature test does not rediscover it.
            影响 / 代价：One debug round (~5 min).来源：Agent · retro.json
            问题 07 · 修复阶段

            Lint sweep found framework-shaped errors in new code (unbound methods, base-to-string, unsafe any, refs during render)

            处理结果未提供

            41 ESLint errors across the new client/server/seed files, including `@typescript-eslint/unbound-method` for destructured `context.repository` in seeds, `no-base-to-string` for CSV/`String(...)` calls, `no-unsafe-*` from `Array.isArray` narrowing to `any[]`, and `react-hooks/refs` from writing a ref during render.

            原因（实现者自述）
            Each is a strict-rule pattern the surrounding code must follow: `MigrationContext.repository` is a method signature (so destructuring detaches `this`); `Array.isArray` narrows to `any[]`; a ref may not be written during render.
            处理方式
            Call `context.repository` off the context object, type `execute<T>()` for query rows, accept `string | number | null | undefined` in `csvEscape`, re-type the array after the `Array.isArray` guard, sync the ref in a `useEffect`, and move the form's pure helpers into `claim-form-model.ts` so the component file only exports components (Fast Refresh).
            影响 / 代价：One long repair round (~35 min).来源：Agent · retro.json
            问题 08 · 修复阶段

            The shadcn-added client packages were moved in package.json but not in pnpm-lock.yaml, so frozen-lockfile verification failed

            本任务已处理

            `pnpm install --frozen-lockfile` failed with `ERR_PNPM_OUTDATED_LOCKFILE`: `@nocobase/i18n (lockfile: 1.0.0-beta.5, manifest: ^1.0.0-beta.5)`. After correcting that, it failed again because `@tanstack/react-table`, `date-fns` and `react-day-picker` sat in the lockfile importer`s `dependencies:` block while package.json declared them in `devDependencies:`.

            原因（实现者自述）
            The shadcn CLI added the client packages to `dependencies` and pinned `@nocobase/i18n` to an exact range; the previous repair moved the three packages to `devDependencies` and restored the `^` in package.json, but never regenerated `pnpm-lock.yaml`, so its importer section still described the pre-move manifest.
            处理方式
            Moved the three entries into the lockfile importer`s `devDependencies:` block in alphabetical position and restored `^1.0.0-beta.5` for `@nocobase/i18n`; confirmed with the exact verification command `pnpm install --frozen-lockfile --lockfile-only --prefer-offline --ignore-scripts`.
            影响 / 代价：One repair round.来源：Agent · retro.json

            实现者补充建议

            来自可选过程笔记，不代表已独立确认；相同建议引用上方评审，其余保留原文。

            建议 01 · Skill 与文档

            Document that a seed must be load-safe: no relative .js import of another source file

            可自动化

            The seed loader rewrites only the entry file's relative import extension, and the in-process app-test path imports raw TypeScript with Node's type stripping. A seed that imports a sibling source module therefore loads via `tsx` but fails inside `createAppTest`, which is exactly where feature tests install seeds.

            建议改动

            Add a short section to the application-development Skill's `references/migrations.md` (seeds): 'A seed is loaded as raw TypeScript; keep it self-contained. Do not import a sibling source module through a `.js` specifier — share nothing, or duplicate the small declaration.' Cross-link it from `references/testing.md`.

            来源：Agent · retro.json

            建议 02 · Skill 与文档

            Document the CSRF trusted-origin requirement for cookie writes in tests

            可自动化

            An app test that writes through a session cookie fails with `INVALID_CSRF_ORIGIN` unless (a) the request carries a trusted `Origin` and (b) that origin is in `auth.trustedOrigins`. `TestSession.fetch` adds no `Origin`, so the two-part fix is not discoverable from the types.

            建议改动

            Add to the application-development Skill's `references/testing.md` (and the authentication plugin Skill) a tested snippet: `createAppTest({ config: { auth: { trustedOrigins: [TEST_ORIGIN] } } })` plus a `write()` helper that sets `origin` and `content-type`, and note that `createAppTest` also needs an explicit `auth.secret`.

            来源：Agent · retro.json

            建议 03 · 脚手架默认值

            createAppTest could provision a test auth secret and trusted origin by default

            可自动化

            Every authenticated feature test must rediscover that the app ships no default secret and that cookie writes need a trusted origin. The harness already provisions an isolated database and config; a default test secret and a trusted `TEST_ORIGIN` would remove a recurring, non-business obstacle.

            建议改动

            In `@nocobase/app-testing` `createTestAppConfig`, default `auth.secret` to a per-test random value and default `auth.trustedOrigins` to `[TEST_ORIGIN]` when unset, leaving both overridable through `config`.

            来源：Agent · retro.json

            建议 04 · Skill 与文档

            Call out that the file plugin's content route is public and must be wrapped for private files

            可自动化

            `defineFileRepositoryApiRoutes` serves `${accessPath}/:file` without authentication: the UUID is the capability. An application storing private documents can, without noticing, expose them to anyone who learns or guesses the UUID.

            建议改动

            In the file plugin Skill's SKILL.md, add a prominent note: the generated content route is public; to protect it, scope an `auth.required()` middleware to your own `accessPath` prefix and record that byte-level per-record authorization is not provided by the plugin.

            来源：Agent · retro.json

            建议 05 · Skill 与文档

            Document the public-base-path rule for hand-built request URLs in app tests

            可自动化

            Only `testApp.request()` adds `publicBasePath`; a root-level URL built by hand must include it or the standalone adapter answers 404. `publicBasePath` is also not a vitest fixture, so a bare reference is `undefined`.

            建议改动

            Add to `references/testing.md`: 'For a root-level path, build `` `http://localhost${testApp.publicBasePath}${path}` `` from the returned app; `publicBasePath` is not a fixture.'

            来源：Agent · retro.json

            建议 06 · 工具与构建

            The shadcn CLI puts client-only packages into dependencies

            需人工判断

            Adding `@nocobase/data-table` and `@nocobase/date-picker` placed `@tanstack/react-table`, `date-fns`, `react-day-picker` and `cn` in `dependencies`, but by the template's deployment split all client imports belong in `devDependencies` (and `@nocobase/i18n` must stay a `^`-ranged dependency).

            建议改动

            Note in the application-development Skill's frontend `references/shadcn.md` that after every `shadcn add` the new client packages must be moved to `devDependencies` and `@nocobase/i18n`'s `^` range restored; consider a post-add check in the CLI.

            来源：Agent · retro.json

            建议 07 · 验收机制

            ESLint strictness is only visible at the end of a feature

            可自动化

            The repository lints with `--max-warnings 0` and several type-aware rules (unbound-method, no-unsafe-*, no-base-to-string) fire on idiomatic framework calls. Running the lint sweep only after the feature is complete produced a 41-error backlog in one round.

            建议改动

            In the implement prompt, ask the agent to run `pnpm exec eslint` on each new file as it is written, or at least once before writing the tests, instead of only at the end.

            来源：Agent · retro.json

            建议 08 · 验收机制

            Regenerate pnpm-lock.yaml after moving a package between dependency sections

            可自动化

            Editing package.json dependency sections without regenerating pnpm-lock.yaml leaves the lockfile importer mismatched. `--frozen-lockfile` is the first verification check, so it aborts the run and masks every later failure (format, lint, typecheck, test, build).

            建议改动

            After any `shadcn add`-driven package.json edit, run `pnpm install --lockfile-only` (or `pnpm install --no-frozen-lockfile`) so the lockfile importer records the correct section, then confirm with `pnpm install --frozen-lockfile --lockfile-only --ignore-scripts`.

            来源：Agent · retro.json

            NocoBase3 库、插件、模板与指引 1

            做得好的地方
            F6 · 路由描述、输入校验与错误语义形成一致的 API 契约

            NocoBase3 内核 · @nocobase/app-server · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/server-routes.md

            ⌄

            @nocobase/app-server/router 把 describeRoute、apiValidator、dataResponse/listResponse 与 ApiError 统一从同一入口导出，输入校验失败自动产生 400 INVALID_ARGUMENT 并逐字段报告，错误状态遵循 AIP-193，指引明确“未声明即缺陷”。

            提供的帮助
            应用能以低歧义方式满足“每个 /api 路由都要文档化并校验”的约束，接口行为一致。
            应保留的能力
            保持现有契约；可在指引中补充更多路径参数与 multipart 的完整示例。
            E35 E36 E37

            问题、误导与建议统一收录在问题与改进，不在此重复展示。

            本次评审的覆盖限制
            • 只读评审：未运行应用、未安装依赖、未修改被评对象；结论基于冻结的 app/、packages/ 与归档日志。

            • 快照仅含可读取的普通文件，未包含 node_modules 运行产物、二进制或超预算文件，不据此确认其实现。

{"type":"turn_end","message":{"role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"The file content route is public (UUID-based) and unauthenticated unless we wrap it. Per AGENTS.md, the requirement is that unauthorized requests must be rejected for private business files. Since invoices are business documents, I should protect the content route. The file Skill says register app-owned auth on `<accessPath>/*` before mounting it. So I need to wrap the root contribution.\n\nBut wrapping a root contribution is tricky: the file plugin's root contribution creates its own router with `router.get(accessPath/:file, ...)`. If I mount that router inside my own root router after installing auth middleware, it should work.\n\nSo plan: in my routes module:\n```ts\nconst fileContributions = defineFileRepositoryApiRoutes({...});\nconst apiRouterFactory = async (app) => {\n  const router = new Hono();\n  const auth = app.container.resolve(authenticationToken);\n  // scoped auth for file CRUD paths\n  for (const path of ['/expenseInvoiceFiles/*']) router.use(path, auth.required());\n  ... business routes ...\n  const apiFile = fileContributions.find(c => c.scope === 'api');\n  router.route('/', await apiFile.createRouter(app));\n  return router;\n};\nconst rootRouterFactory = async (app) => {\n  const router = new Hono();\n  const auth = app.container.resolve(authenticationToken);\n  router.use('/uploads/expenseInvoices/*', auth.required());\n  const rootFile = fileContributions.find(c => c.scope === 'root');\n  router.route('/', await rootFile.createRouter(app));\n  return router;\n};\nexport default [defineApiRoutes(apiRouterFactory), defineRootRoutes(rootRouterFactory)];\n```\n\nWait, but the *content route* on the root scope is mounted at the root. But then `router.route('/', subRouter)` where the subRouter has `router.get('/uploads/expenseInvoices/:file')`.             E35 · packages/@nocobase/app-server/dist/router/api-error.js:10–70

            AIP-193 风格状态码映射与 ApiError：INVALID_ARGUMENT/UNAUTHENTICATED/PERMISSION_DENIED/... 及 reason/domain/fieldViolations。

            export const apiErrorStatusCodes = Object.freeze({
    INVALID_ARGUMENT: 400,
    FAILED_PRECONDITION: 400,
    OUT_OF_RANGE: 400,
    UNAUTHENTICATED: 401,
    PERMISSION_DENIED: 403,
    NOT_FOUND: 404,
    ALREADY_EXISTS: 409,
    ABORTED: 409,
    RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED: 429,
    INTERNAL: 500,
    UNIMPLEMENTED: 501,
    UNAVAILABLE: 503,
    DEADLINE_EXCEEDED: 504,
});
/** The error reasons and domain the framework itself reports. Plugins use their own domain and reasons. */
export const appErrorDomain = 'app';
/**
 * An error a route throws to answer with the standard `/api` error body.
 *
 * ```ts
 * throw new ApiError({ status: 'NOT_FOUND', reason: 'APP_NOT_FOUND', domain: 'hub', message: `App ${id} was not found.` });
 * ```
 */
export class ApiError extends Error {
    /** The HTTP status of the response. */
    code;
    status;
    reason;
    domain;
    localizedMessage;
    fieldViolations;
    metadata;
    constructor(options) {
        super(options.message, options.cause === undefined ? undefined : { cause: options.cause });
        this.name = 'ApiError';
        this.code = options.httpStatus ?? apiErrorStatusCodes[options.status];
        this.status = options.status;
        this.reason = options.reason;
        this.domain = options.domain;
        this.localizedMessage = options.localizedMessage;
        this.fieldViolations = options.fieldViolations;
        this.metadata = options.metadata;
    }
    toPayload(requestId) {
        return {
            code: this.code,
            status: this.status,
            reason: this.reason,
            domain: this.domain,
            message: this.message,
            ...(this.localizedMessage
                ? { localizedMessage: this.localizedMessage }
                : {}),
            ...(this.fieldViolations?.length
                ? { fieldViolations: this.fieldViolations }
                : {}),
            ...(this.metadata ? { metadata: this.metadata } : {}),
            ...(requestId ? { requestId } : {}),
        };
    }

            原文件 SHA-256：25b0936828fa29863438e7a0adf32f35cbe75b1d1cdcf4bcc7985876f3e3c076

            E36 · packages/@nocobase/app-server/dist/router/openapi/describe.js:77–133

            apiValidator 复用 Hono validator 以产出 400 INVALID_ARGUMENT，并把输入 schema 声明进 API 文档；dataResponse 生成 { data } 响应文档。

            export function apiValidator(target, schema, options) {
    const middleware = validator(target, schema, (result) => {
        if (!result.success)
            throw invalidApiInputError(result.error);
    }, options);
    // Document the schema the way the response helpers do, rather than with hono-openapi's own conversion.
    const metadata = middleware[uniqueSymbol];
    const input = resolver(schema, 'input');
    metadata.toJSONSchema = input.toJSONSchema;
    metadata.toOpenAPISchema = async () => {
        const converted = await convertApiSchema(schema, 'input');
        return target === 'header' ? withoutIgnoredHeaders(converted) : converted;
    };
    return middleware;
}
function isStandardSchema(schema) {
    return typeof schema === 'object' && schema !== null && '~standard' in schema;
}
async function resolveParts(parts) {
    const schemas = {};
    let components;
    for (const [key, part] of Object.entries(parts)) {
        if (!isStandardSchema(part)) {
            schemas[key] = part;
            continue;
        }
        // Responses describe what the server sends, so a schema with a transform documents its output.
        const result = await convertApiSchema(part, 'output');
        schemas[key] = result.schema;
        const collected = { ...result.components?.schemas };
        if (Object.keys(collected).length > 0) {
            components = {
                ...components,
                schemas: { ...components?.schemas, ...collected },
            };
        }
    }
    return { schemas, components };
}
/**
 * A `resolver()`-shaped value whose OpenAPI schema is built from several schemas, each a Standard Schema or a plain
 * OpenAPI schema, resolved when the document is generated.
 */
function composeSchema(parts, build) {
    return {
        vendor: 'nocobase',
        validate: (value) => ({ value }),
        toJSONSchema: async () => build((await resolveParts(parts)).schemas),
        toOpenAPISchema: async () => {
            const resolved = await resolveParts(parts);
            return {
                schema: build(resolved.schemas),
                components: resolved.components,
            };
        },
    };
}

            E38 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/http-api.md:276–288

            http-api.md：手写 /api 路由必须 describeRoute()，响应用 dataResponse/listResponse/emptyResponse 与 apiErrorResponse，全部从 @nocobase/app-server/router 导入。

            Every hand-written `/api` route declares itself with `describeRoute()` from `@nocobase/app-server/router`, as in the example above, after its authentication and permission middleware and before its `apiValidator()`s. A route that declares nothing is a defect the API check reports by name.

- **`tags`**: the plugin name in PascalCase, such as `Hub`, `Users` or `AiEmployee`. The application's own routes tag the resource, such as `Orders`.
- **`summary`**: an English verb phrase, such as `Deploy an app`.
- **`operationId`**: the namespace, a verb and the resource in camelCase, unique across the application, such as `hubDeployApp`; the application's own routes have no namespace, `cancelOrder`.
- **`description`**: what a caller cannot read from the schemas, such as the permission a route requires or which credential it expects. A credential that is not one of the document's security schemes is described here and only here, as the Hub's routes say which ones also accept a Hub publishing key in `Authorization: Bearer hub_app_…`.
- **Inputs**: `apiValidator()` for every path, query, header and JSON input, which documents the parameters and the body.
- **Responses**: `dataResponse(schema)` for `{ data }`, `listResponse(itemSchema, metaSchema?)` for `{ data, meta }`, `emptyResponse()` for a `204`, and the standard error body through `apiErrorResponse(status)` or `apiErrorResponse(409, 'When …')`. Do not list `400` for input validation: a route that uses `apiValidator` gets the `400` automatically. List `400` yourself only for another reason, such as a failed precondition. `apiErrorResponses` is `401`, `403` and `500`, for an authenticated route with a permission check; otherwise list each status the route can return with `apiErrorResponse(code)`.
- **Security**: a route a caller reaches without a credential, such as a status probe or the locales the sign-in page reads, declares `security: []`. Every other route inherits the document's top-level requirement, a session cookie (`cookieAuth`) or an API key in `x-api-key` (`apiKeyAuth`), which the authentication and API keys plugins contribute.
- **Schemas** live in the plugin's `server/routes/schemas.ts`. A schema several routes share carries `.meta({ ref: '<PluginName><Thing>' })`, such as `HubDeployment`, and becomes a named component; public fields carry `.meta({ description })`, which stays next to the `$ref` when the field's schema is a shared one. A response object is documented open, so adding a field later is not a breaking change, unless its schema is `z.strictObject()`; a JSON body validated with `z.strictObject()` is documented closed. Recursive schemas such as `z.json()` need no workaround: they become components, named by their `ref` when they declare one.
- **Response schemas describe what the handler returns.** Read the handler and the service it calls rather than guessing, and annotate the schema with the service's view type, `export const OrderSchema: z.ZodType<OrderView> = z.object({ ... })`, so that a response and its documentation cannot drift apart without failing `typecheck`.

Import all of these from `@nocobase/app-server/router`, never from `hono-openapi`: the declarations are attached to middleware under a symbol that module owns, and only the application's copy generates the document. A plugin that declares `hono-openapi` fails `pnpm peers:check`.

            原文件 SHA-256：5ffc49ce2d72ce9cbfff24a63a42a13d9879c878b87e093bb64f504301f9a8b2

            E39 · packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/migration/internal/task-loader.js:1–12

            task-loader 的 __rewriteRelativeImportExtension 只对传入的入口文件路径做 .ts/.tsx 扩展名改写，DEFAULT_TASK_EXTENSIONS 含 .ts。

            var __rewriteRelativeImportExtension = (this && this.__rewriteRelativeImportExtension) || function (path, preserveJsx) {
    if (typeof path === "string" && /^\.\.?\//.test(path)) {
        return path.replace(/\.(tsx)$|((?:\.d)?)((?:\.[^./]+?)?)\.([cm]?)ts$/i, function (m, tsx, d, ext, cm) {
            return tsx ? preserveJsx ? ".jsx" : ".js" : d && (!ext || !cm) ? m : (d + ext + "." + cm.toLowerCase() + "js");
        });
    }
    return path;
};
import { readdir } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { basename, extname } from 'node:path';
import { pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';
export const DEFAULT_TASK_EXTENSIONS = ['.js', '.mjs', '.cjs', '.ts'];

            E40 · packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/migration/internal/task-loader.js:50–62

            importTaskDefinition 仅对入口 URL 调用 __rewriteRelativeImportExtension 后 import；其内部相对导入的解析不由该函数处理。

                }
    return extensions.has(extname(fileName));
}
export function taskNameFromFileName(fileName) {
    return basename(fileName, extname(fileName));
}
/** The mtime query keeps a re-read after an edit from serving a cached module. */
export async function importTaskDefinition(filePath, mtimeMs) {
    const url = pathToFileURL(filePath);
    url.searchParams.set('mtime', String(Math.trunc(mtimeMs)));
    const module = await import(__rewriteRelativeImportExtension(url.href));
    return module.default;
}

            E41 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:129–141

            nocobase-db Skill 的绝对规则：迁移/种子必须自包含，不得导入会持续演进的 Collection 定义或共享常量；种子必须幂等。

            The rules are absolute, because these files are history that has already run on other machines:

- **Self-contained.** Spell out every table, field, index, constraint and metadata operation in the file. Never import a Collection definition, model, registry or shared constant that keeps evolving — that silently changes both the behavior and the checksum of something already applied.
- **Immutable once merged.** Check with `git log -- <file>` if unsure. Before the branch is merged you may correct the file in place; after it, every change is a new, later file. Never hard-code a previous checksum to make an edited migration look untouched. Correcting it in place changes nothing on its own — it is recorded as executed, so a plain apply skips it and the database keeps the schema the old file produced. `pnpm nocobase db redo` rolls the latest batch back and applies it again.
- **The file name body matches `name`**, and the name is unique across every source in the application.
- **Declare `irreversible: true`** when there is no real reverse operation, instead of writing a `down()` that quietly does nothing.
- **A seed is idempotent**, keyed on a stable business value backed by a unique constraint, so a repeated run is a no-op — `upsertOne` is the direct way to express that. A seed context has no `builder` at all, and a seed has no rollback or truncate behavior.

A backfill belongs in the migration that makes it necessary, using `query` from the same context — not in a seed.

Both run inside a transaction by default. `transaction: false` opts out, and a failure then leaves partial work behind with no history record, so take it only when the operation genuinely cannot run in one.

A task's `connection` is not the runtime connection either. It carries `dialect`, `capabilities` and `client()` — enough to branch on the database, and deliberately not a way back to the manager. Anything conditional on the dialect reads it from there.

            原文件 SHA-256：d33a58740d6a2921689e56f964c081354574ed857389118e319719bde6936991

            E42 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/migrations.md:15–32

            migrations.md：db apply 先应用待执行迁移再应用待执行种子；种子写入的结构须先由迁移建立；多连接的目录与命令约定。

            ```

This applies pending migrations and then pending seeds for `database.default`, including registered plugins, ordered by name across all sources. Both halves run only what is pending, so on an already-migrated database it applies seeds alone. The template defaults to `main`. Plugin migrations, seeds and runtime default reads/writes always use this same connection; changing `database.default` changes the application system database.

Seeds are the second half of that run, so the structure a seed writes into must already exist from an earlier migration. Keep seed data fixed and reproducible — no current timestamps or random values in identifying fields — and never let a repeat run silently overwrite data a user has edited.

## Multiple connections

Use `database/<connectionName>/migrations` and `database/<connectionName>/seeds`. Each name must match a configured connection and contain only letters, digits, underscores or hyphens. Directories do not register connections. A connection without application-owned database source needs no empty directory.

Configure `connections.<name>.migrations` and `.seeds` in the database section. Each accepts `autoRun`, `directory` or `sources`, `packageName`, `tableName`, `lockTableName`, and `extensions`. Explicit paths resolve relative to the application root and must exist when executed. Without an explicit path the application resolves the per-connection directory under its runtime database directory, including `dist/database` after compilation. Custom sources replace the application's conventional source; registered plugin sources are still added only on the default connection.

The default connection runs migrations and seeds automatically unless disabled; other connections default to `autoRun: false`. Startup runs the default connection first, then other names in stable ascending order, migrations before seeds within each connection. A failure stops startup; completed database changes remain committed. No cross-connection transaction or rollback is provided.

```bash
pnpm nocobase db apply --connection analytics
pnpm nocobase db apply --all --json
```

The declarations used here are in [the sales workflow](business-module.md). Register `sales.prepared` and `sales.region` before serving requests. A page grant can equally be written as `authz.pages.grant('sales.quotes')` where the service is available; a seed has no service, so it writes the plain grant. Use `{ key, ns }` titles and matching client translations in a localized product; plain titles here keep the snippets small. Grants store record access keys and parameters, never resolver functions. Each business data scope value is a record access key or a record selection; `''` selects nothing.

## A normal App seed has a database context

`defineSeed` from `@nocobase/db` receives `{ query, connection }`. `connection` is a restricted `SeedConnection`, not a complete runtime `DatabaseConnection`; the seed has no application container. Do not cast it to construct an App authorization service or assume `authorizationToken` can be resolved there.

For initial installation, a seed may write the documented persistence rows below after the owning plugins' migrations. Keep this adapter confined to installation data, use fluent values as its input, and test against the installed schema. Runtime routes must use the services instead. Keep one seed-data module per table.

```ts
import { defineSeed } from '@nocobase/db';
import { encodeAuthorizationTitle } from '@nocobase/authorization/core';
import { engineer } from '../../seed-data/permission-sets.js';

export default defineSeed({
  name: '202609190001_sales_jobs',
  transaction: true,
  async run({ query }) {
    const existing = await query
      .selectFrom('authorizationPermissionSets')
      .select('id')
      .where('key', '=', engineer.key)
      .executeTakeFirst();
    if (existing) return; // Preserve an administrator's existing definition.
    const now = new Date();
    await query
      .insertInto('authorizationPermissionSets')
      .values({
        id: engineer.key,
        key: engineer.key,
        title: encodeAuthorizationTitle(engineer.title),
        grants: JSON.stringify(engineer.grants),
        createdAt: now,
        updatedAt: now,
      })
      .execute();
  },
});
```

This seed lives in `database/main/seeds/202609190001_sales_jobs.ts` and imports `database/seed-data/permission-sets.ts`. Adapt the path to the App's configured seed directory; the seed name matches the filename. Ensure schema migrations for authorization and business collections have run, and explicitly order prerequisite installation data. Use the App's existing migration/seed commands. Do not run this code in a migration: migrations must describe fixed schema history and cannot import live business declarations.

### Persist initial permission-set assignments

            E46 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/server/auth.js:125–162

            app-plugin-authentication 的 checkCookieWriteOrigin：携带 cookie 的非 GET/HEAD/OPTIONS 请求必须带可信 Origin/Referer，否则拒绝（INVALID_CSRF_ORIGIN）。

                /** Protect writes authenticated by a browser cookie, including routes that skip normal session lookup. */
    async checkCookieWriteOrigin(context) {
        if (['GET', 'HEAD', 'OPTIONS'].includes(context.req.method))
            return;
        // A credential header is not proof that it was used: an invalid API key may fall back to a valid cookie.
        // Cookie-free API key requests have no ambient browser credential to forge.
        if (!context.req.header('cookie'))
            return;
        const authContext = await this.auth.$context;
        const origin = context.req.header('origin');
        const inferredBaseURL = origin === 'null' &&
            context.req.header('sec-fetch-site') === 'same-origin'
            ? getBaseURL(undefined, authContext.options.basePath, context.req.raw, false, authContext.options.advanced?.trustedProxyHeaders)
            : undefined;
        const source = (inferredBaseURL ? getOrigin(inferredBaseURL) : undefined) ??
            origin ??
            context.req.header('referer');
        if (!source || source === 'null') {
            return invalidCsrfOrigin(context);
        }
        // Better Auth also accepts per-request trusted origins. Its static context contains the
        // configured base URL, static origins, plugin origins, and BETTER_AUTH_TRUSTED_ORIGINS.
        const configuredOrigins = authContext.options.trustedOrigins;
        const mergedOrigins = typeof configuredOrigins === 'function'
            ? await configuredOrigins(context.req.raw)
            : (configuredOrigins ?? []);
        const requestAuthContext = Object.create(authContext);
        requestAuthContext.trustedOrigins = [
            ...authContext.trustedOrigins,
            ...mergedOrigins.filter((origin) => typeof origin === 'string' && Boolean(origin)),
        ];
        const trustedByAuth = requestAuthContext.isTrustedOrigin(source, {
            allowRelativePaths: false,
        });
        if (!trustedByAuth) {
            return invalidCsrfOrigin(context);
        }
    }

            E47 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/server/testing.js:19–50

            测试助手 signIn()/TestSession：DEFAULT_ADMIN_CREDENTIALS 与 TEST_ORIGIN，但返回的 fetch 只带 cookie/头，不设置 Origin，导致后续 cookie 写请求被 CSRF 校验拒绝。

            export async function signIn(app, credentials) {
    const apiRoot = `${TEST_ORIGIN}${trimTrailingSlash(app.publicBasePath)}/api`;
    const byUsername = 'username' in credentials;
    const response = await app.fetch(new Request(`${apiRoot}/auth/sign-in/${byUsername ? 'username' : 'email'}`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify(credentials),
    }));
    if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(`Sign-in as "${byUsername ? credentials.username : credentials.email}" failed with ${response.status}: ${await response.text()}`);
    }
    const body = (await response.json());
    if (!body.user) {
        throw new Error('Sign-in succeeded without returning the signed-in user.');
    }
    const cookie = response.headers
        .getSetCookie()
        .map((header) => header.split(';')[0])
        .join('; ');
    return {
        user: body.user,
        cookie,
        fetch: async (path, init = {}) => {
            const headers = new Headers(init.headers);
            headers.set('cookie', [headers.get('cookie'), cookie].filter(Boolean).join('; '));
            return app.fetch(new Request(path.startsWith('/') ? `${apiRoot}${path}` : path, {
                ...init,
                headers,
            }));
        },
    };
}

            ## Testing through the whole application

To check a route's authentication and permission boundaries as a user meets them, start the application itself on test databases with `@nocobase/app-testing/server` and sign in through its own sign-in route with `@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/testing`:

```ts
import {
  DEFAULT_ADMIN_CREDENTIALS,
  signIn,
} from '@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/testing';
import { createAppTest } from '@nocobase/app-testing/server';
import { createStandaloneServer } from '../../server/standalone.ts';

const test = createAppTest({ createServer: createStandaloneServer });

test('lists orders for an administrator only', async ({ testApp, request }) => {
  expect((await request('/orders')).status).toBe(401);
  const admin = await signIn(testApp, DEFAULT_ADMIN_CREDENTIALS);
  expect((await admin.fetch('/orders')).status).toBe(200);
});

            E49 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/testing.md:108–118

            testing.md：测试不自选数据库，由 @nocobase/app-testing 提供；并指向迁移测试 describeMigration 与前端测试参考。

            ## Test databases

A test never chooses its database: it does not import a `@nocobase/db-<dialect>` package, configure `dialect: 'sqlite'` or `':memory:'`, or reach for SQL only one database understands, such as `PRAGMA` or `sqlite_master`. It gets its databases from `@nocobase/app-testing/server` — `createAppTest()` for the whole application, `createTestDatabase()` or `createDatabaseTest()` for a database alone — on the dialect `NOCOBASE_TEST_DB_DIALECT` names, and SQLite when it is unset, so `pnpm test` needs no server. Assert on the schema with `expectCollection()`, which compares Field and Collection names rather than physical ones.

## Testing the frontend

Component tests, the route test and translation checks are described in [frontend tests](frontend/references/testing.md), including a minimal component test that renders with the real i18n runtime from `@nocobase/i18n/testing`. Do not mock `@nocobase/i18n/client`: a mocked `t` hides misspelt keys and wrong namespaces.

## Testing migrations

Run against a real test database. A test that only imports the migration file proves nothing about the schema it produces. `describeMigration()` from `@nocobase/app-testing/server` applies the migrations before it, applies this one, rolls it back and applies it again, and checks after each step that metadata and tables agree and that rolling back restores every table as it was; its `up` and `down` callbacks verify the fields, indexes and constraints with `expectCollection()`.

/** Builds a definition; `authz.recordAccess.define` registers it. */
export function defineRecordAccess(key, configure) {
    if (!key)
        throw new TypeError('A record access key is required');
    return configure(new RecordAccessBuilder({ key, collections: [] }));
}
function validateDefinition(definition) {
    if (!definition.key)
        throw new TypeError('A record access key is required');
    if (typeof definition.resolve !== 'function')
        throw new TypeError('Record access requires a resolver');
    if (!definition.collections.length ||
        definition.collections.some((name) => typeof name !== 'string' || !name))
        throw new TypeError('Record access requires collections');
    return definition;
}

            E52 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/authorization.md:22–31

            authorization.md：每个 server/routes/ 端点都要装认证与授权；业务操作对复合资源授权并绑定 repository.withPolicy；页面 authz 声明与 useCan 用法。

            Every `server/routes/` endpoint installs authentication and authorization. A business operation checks `{ resource: { type: 'composite', id }, action }` with the request context's `authorize` once and binds each returned `conditions.database` policy through `repository.withPolicy`. Do not replace it with collection-level authorization or an in-memory filter. Use `authz.database.policyFor` for collection CRUD and `authz.database.authorizeRepository` for simple business Repository routes, mapping each method to its composite action. The authorization Skill documents these choices and their contracts.

Declare `authz` on the first page of every path in `client/routes.ts`: `{ resource: { type: 'page', id }, action: 'access' }` for a business page, the page's `settings` item for a settings page, `'unrestricted'` for a root-only page, or `'skip'` explicitly; nothing is inferred from the route name. Nested pages inherit it; an omitted value on a first page defaults to `'unrestricted'` for protected App and settings pages and `'skip'` otherwise, with a development warning. Use `useCan({ resource, action })` for button visibility and server-computed eligibility for record-sensitive actions. Keep pending/failed checks closed and refresh after permission changes. Client visibility does not enforce the API. The authorization Skill’s `references/client-development.md` includes the component, relation controls and settings lifecycle; all required APIs are bundled in that Skill rather than external package documentation.

The permission workspace edits page grants, business actions with their data scopes, and settings items. Field and relation capabilities are code-owned. A new field or workflow must update its declaration and enforcement; it is not an administrator entering arbitrary database grants. Follow the dedicated Skill for custom record access, multi-table transactions, relation targets, protected assignments and last-administrator checks.

## Verify and explain

Test an ordinary user with no grant, page-only and action-only grants, rows owned by another user, forbidden fields, invalid relation targets, and stale workflow states. For multi-table operations, remove each required scope independently. Test direct API requests, rollback and independent revocation of direct/inherited sources. Use the inspector to understand decisions, not as a substitute for running the operation.

                /**
     * The Policy every action of this exposure runs under, including uploads.
     *
     * `uploadOne` and `uploadMany` do not go through `defineRepositoryApiRoutes`
     * — they compose their own values and call the Repository directly — so what
     * they bind is derived from this one: the `create` scope and defaults are
     * inherited, and the field allowlist is replaced by the file columns, which
     * is the only thing a caller cannot influence here. See `uploadPolicy`.
     *
     * The public byte route under `accessPath` is the deliberate exception. It
     * serves anyone holding the UUID, has no authentication of its own, and is
     * unaffected by this Policy.
     */

    readonly actions: FileRepositoryApiActions;
}

    readonly publicBasePath?: string;

    readonly principal?: (context: Context) => P | Promise<P>;
}
