            E55 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-file/dist/server/repository.js:15–50

            app-plugin-file：uploadPolicy 将 create 的 scope/defaults 继承、字段替换为文件列，read 固定 scope:true 服务上传回执与公开字节路由；update/delete 恒为 false。

                'createdAt',
    'updatedAt',
];
/**
 * The Policy the upload path binds, derived from the exposure's own.
 *
 * It is derived rather than used as it stands because an upload supplies no
 * caller fields at all — `store()` composes every value — so a field allowlist
 * has nothing to constrain here and the plugin substitutes its own columns.
 * What is inherited is exactly the two dimensions that do not describe caller
 * input: which rows this principal may produce (`scope`), and what the server
 * stamps onto them (`defaults`). A `create` that is `false` stays `false`, so
 * an exposure that forbids creating rows forbids uploading them too.
 *
 * This is the only place a Policy is widened, and the reason is specific to
 * this path. It licenses nothing elsewhere.
 */
export function uploadPolicy(base) {
    const create = base.create === true || base.create === false
        ? base.create
        : {
            scope: base.create.scope,
            defaults: base.create.defaults,
            fields: FILE_COLUMNS,
            relations: {},
        };
    // `read` serves this path's own returning record and the public byte route,
    // both of which need every column. `update` and `delete` never run here.
    return {
        create,
        read: { scope: true, fields: FILE_COLUMNS, relations: {} },
        update: false,
        delete: false,
    };
}
export class FileRepositoryError extends Error {

            E56 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-file/SKILL.md:122–128

            file Skill：policy 治理 exposure 的所有动作；accessPath/<uuid> 内容路由在 /api 之外；这些是公开路由，需应用自挂持路径前缀的认证与授权，且不得 use('*')。

            `policy` is required and governs every action of the exposure, uploads included. It is a Repository Policy: `read`, `create`, `update` and `delete`, each `true`, `false`, or a rule node. A node that is `false` refuses that operation; a node with no `fields` accepts no caller-supplied field, which is what a metadata `createOne` needs before it will accept anything. Pass a function of the principal — `policy: (principal) => ({ ... })` — together with a `principal(context)` resolver to scope rows to the caller; without a principal the request is refused with 403.

An upload supplies no caller fields, so it binds a Policy derived from this one: the `create` scope and defaults are inherited and the field allowlist is replaced by the file columns. `create: false` therefore forbids uploading as well as creating metadata — decided, with the principal, before the body limit, the content type or the multipart body is read, so a refused upload answers `403 WRITE_FORBIDDEN` (domain `app`) and stores nothing — and a `create.defaults` of `{ ownerId }` is stamped onto uploaded rows — which is what keeps an uploaded file inside the same scope `findMany` reads. The content route under `accessPath` is the exception: it is public and this Policy does not reach it.

Content is GET `<accessPath>/<uuid>.<ext>` outside `/api`, omitting the dot when extensionless. Stream returns full bytes as an attachment; redirect returns a public storage URL or a five-minute signed URL. A disk without URL support requires stream mode; there is no automatic fallback.

These are public routes. For restricted files, register App-owned authentication and authorization on the paths each contribution owns before mounting it — `/<name>/<action>` per exposed API action, `<accessPath>/*` for content. Never `router.use('*', ...)` in a contribution router: contributions share the mounted router, so it also guards the SPA and every contribution mounted after yours. Check the operation and record/parent-record access; a login page, private disk, Client filter or Policy field allowlist is not authentication. Add scoped middleware and principal-based Policy to the generated routes first. If an existing contract needs an adapter, extend that module using the public Server manager and retain its authorization checks; missing built-in authentication alone is not a reason to replace the routes. Never trust browser-supplied ownership.

    try {
        server = await createServer({
            viteDevUrl: false,
            ...serverOptions,
            configPath: config.path,
            env: {
                APP_STORAGE_DIR: path.join(config.directory, 'storage'),
                ...serverOptions.env,
            },
        });
    }
    catch (error) {
        await config.dispose();
        throw error;
    }
    const { application } = server;
    const database = application.container.resolve(databaseManagerToken);
    const apiRoot = `http://localhost${trimTrailingSlash(application.publicBasePath)}/api`;
    const fetch = (request) => server.fetch(request);
    return {
        server,
        application,
        publicBasePath: application.publicBasePath,
        fetch,
        database,
        connection: database.connection(),
        config,
        request: async (target, init) => fetch(new Request(target.startsWith('/') ? `${apiRoot}${target}` : target, init)),
        close: async () => {
            try {
                await server.close();
            }
            finally {
                await config.dispose();
            }
        },
    };
}

            原文件 SHA-256：e23ef4e4041692cb11bed9b42ad845fcaabdebc798bb3dd9a81d6489abf2005e

            E60 · app/vitest.config.ts:18–32

            模板 vitest 配置：include 为 tests/**/*.test.{ts,tsx}，setupFiles 只引入 client-config，未开启 globals。

                server: {
      deps: {
        // Transform the public Client surfaces so vi.mock applies, without duplicating the Server packages' identity tokens.
        inline: [
          /@nocobase\/app-plugin-authorization\/(?:dist\/)?client\//u,
          /@nocobase\/app-plugin-notification-in-app\/(?:dist\/)?client\//u,
        ],
      },
    },
    // A glob rather than a list of filenames. The list had to be edited by hand for every test added or removed and
    // silently drifted: it named a file that no longer existed while several real test files were absent from it, so
    // those tests were never run at all.
    include: ['tests/**/*.test.{ts,tsx}'],
    setupFiles: ['./tests/setup/client-config.ts'],
    // Keep the command usable if an application intentionally removes all scaffold tests before adding its own.

            Business delivery context

            业务交付与验收背景

            已提交 PR · 等待人工评审

            小公司报销系统通过端到端验收：员工填费用明细、传发票、自动合计并提交，主管审批，超5000元转财务复核，通过后财务登记付款；角色可见范围、驳回重提、付款锁定、审计轨迹、首页统计与批量导出均符合需求。

            查看 PR #658 ↗查看需求 ↗本轮运行与原始产物 ↗查看验收依据

            What was delivered

            业务场景与交付说明

            申请与自动合计

            员工可新增多条费用明细并上传发票（PNG/PDF/XLSX），合计随明细实时计算；提交后进入主管审批，历史记录提交人、时间与状态迁移。

            分级审批与付款

            主管只处理本部门申请；金额超过5000元经主管审批后自动转财务复核，财务通过后方可登记付款；未超阈值仅主管审批即可。全流程保留操作人、时间与意见。

            权限与可见范围

            员工仅见本人报销且无审批权，主管仅见本部门申请，财务可见全部；非管理员无法进入后台管理页面。

            驳回、锁定与统计导出

            驳回必须填写原因，申请人可修改后重提；付款后费用明细不可编辑；首页按月份与部门统计支出；批量导出显示0%-100%进度且等待期间可继续其他操作。

            01员工填写费用明细并上传发票，系统自动计算合计
            →
            02员工提交，状态变为待主管审批
            →
            03部门主管审批：未超5000元直接通过
            →
            04超过5000元自动转财务负责人复核
            →
            05财务复核通过后登记付款信息
            →
            06状态变为已付款，费用明细锁定不可编辑
            →
            07首页按月份与部门汇总支出
            →
            08批量导出报销资料并显示后台进度

            业务顺序示意，操作证据见逐项验收。

            验收记录

            每项直接显示结果；展开可看原始要求、操作记录、观察结果和截图。证据批注单独显示，不改写原始验收状态。

            01

            我是财务负责人，要搭建一个小公司的报销系统。员工填写费用明细、上传发票，系统自动计算合计；先由部门主管审批，超过5000元还需财务负责人复核，全部通过后由财务登记付款。 员工只看自己的报销，主管处理本部门申请，财务查看全部。驳回必须说明原因，可以修改后重提；付款后不能修改费用明细。每次提交、审批和付款保留操作人、时间及意见。首页按月份和部门统计支出；批量导出报销资料时显示进度，等待期间可以继续处理其他工作。

            非阻塞性观察（不影响业务结论）：Home 的 By month 表头存在未替换文案“{{count}} claims”；上传损坏的 corrupt.png 被接受为 Done 且预览 naturalWidth=0，未显示显式失败反馈；不支持格式 unsupported.exe 因 accept 过滤未进入列表，也无显式提示。控制台与 page errors 均为空。

            A1 · 11 条操作 · 10 条观察 · 10 张证据
            Agent 报告通过
            ⌄

            原始验收要求

            我是财务负责人，要搭建一个小公司的报销系统。员工填写费用明细、上传发票，系统自动计算合计；先由部门主管审批，超过5000元还需财务负责人复核，全部通过后由财务登记付款。 员工只看自己的报销，主管处理本部门申请，财务查看全部。驳回必须说明原因，可以修改后重提；付款后不能修改费用明细。每次提交、审批和付款保留操作人、时间及意见。首页按月份和部门统计支出；批量导出报销资料时显示进度，等待期间可以继续处理其他工作。

            实际操作

            1. 管理员 nocobase 登录后进入 Reimbursements，确认业务界面；用管理员 UI 通过两次操作完成全流程复核。
            2. 以示例员工 张三(zhangsan) 登录：首页显示 3 笔本人报销（研发部 Engineering），列表只出现本人单号，详情页无 Approve/Reject；确认员工只看自己且无审批权。
            3. 以员工 王五(wangwu) 新建报销单 EX20261007EC024A：填写 Purpose/Note，新增两条费用明细 Travel 4200 + Meals 1500，上传 sample.pdf 与 sample.xlsx，页面 Total 自动计算为 5700.00 并提示超过 5000 需财务复核；Save and submit 提交成功，历史记录显示 王五 submitted · Draft → Awaiting supervisor。
            4. 在编辑已驳回单 EX202602180004 时验证原值回填：Purpose=培训报名费 Training fee、Category=Other、Amount=2300.00、费用说明=行业培训课程 Industry training course、日期 February 17th 2026；修改金额为 2600 并上传 sample.png，Total 实时变为 2600.00，Save and resubmit 后状态 Rejected → Awaiting supervisor，金额更新为 2600.00 且发票可预览为 sample.png。
            5. 以主管 赵六(zhaoliu) 登录：列表只显示其负责的销售部/市场部申请，看不到研发部张三的单据；打开 5700 单点击 Approve 填写意见“主管审批通过 Supervisor approved”，状态 Awaiting supervisor → Awaiting finance（>5000 未直接通过）；该单在主管视角不再出现审批按钮。
            6. 以财务 钱七(qianqi) 登录：列表可见全部部门 8 笔单据；打开 5700 单点击 Approve 填写意见“财务复核通过 Finance review passed”，状态 Awaiting finance → Approved，随后 Register payment 选择 Corporate card 并填付款备注，状态 → Paid；另对 900 单（<5000）直接由主管 Approved 后登记 Cash 付款，验证阈值分支。
            7. 财务对 Awaiting finance 单点击 Reject 空提交，出现“A rejection must state a reason.”校验，确认驳回必须说明原因；对已驳回单由申请人在编辑页修改后重提，验证可修改后重提。
            8. 打开已付款单 EX202603010005（Paid）：详情仅 Details/History，无 Edit 按钮；对比被驳回/待审单存在 Edit，确认付款后不能修改费用明细。
            9. 在列表 Batch export 点击 Start export，观察到 Queued… 0% → Ready 100%（7 of 7 claims），点击 Download CSV 对应下载接口返回 200 text/csv（附件 expenses-2026-10-07.csv，10 行费用明细）；等待导出期间切换到 Home 再返回，导出任务仍保留为 Ready，验证后台进度与可继续工作。
            10. 切换各角色后完整重新加载应用，并直接访问 /main/settings/users：财务/员工均显示“No settings available”，确认非管理员无后台权限。
            11. 检查浏览器 console 与 page errors：均为空；另用清单文件测试损坏/不支持格式发票。

            观察结果

            1. 员工可见性：zhangsan 首页/列表仅 3 笔本人单据，wangwu 仅 4 笔本人单据，详情无审批按钮；主管 zhaoliu 仅见销售部+市场部单据；财务 qianqi 见全部部门 8 笔。
            2. 自动合计：新增两条 4200+1500 明细后页面 Total=5700.00，提交后服务端 detail TOTAL=5700.00；编辑 2600 后 Total=2600.00 并保存成功。
            3. 阈值分支：5700 与示例 6800 均经 Awaiting supervisor → Awaiting finance →（财务 Approve）Approved →（付款）Paid；900 单 Awaiting supervisor → Approved（无财务复核）→ Paid。
            4. 驳回必填原因：空提交出现“A rejection must state a reason.”；已驳回单 Detail 顶部显示驳回原因“发票信息不完整，请补充后重新提交”。
            5. 付款锁定：Paid 单详情无 Edit；被驳回单与待审单有 Edit/Submit for approval。
            6. 审计轨迹：History 完整记录 提交(Draft→Awaiting supervisor)、主管审批(Awaiting supervisor→Awaiting finance)、财务复核(Awaiting finance→Approved)、付款(Approved→Paid)，均含操作人、时间与意见，例如“赵六 Zhao Liu approved it / 钱七 Qian Qi registered the payment”。
            7. 首页统计：Home 显示 By month（January/February/March/October 2026 的 Claimed 与 Paid out）与 By department（销售部/研发部/市场部 金额与占比）以及 By status 计数与金额。
            8. 批量导出：Start export 显示 Queued… 0% 进度，随后 Ready 100% / 7 of 7 claims；Download CSV 返回 content-disposition: attachment; filename=expenses-2026-10-07.csv，10 行明细；导出进行时切换页面后任务仍在。
            9. 发票上传与预览：sample.pdf、sample.xlsx、sample.png 上传成功并可在 Invoice 单元格 Preview，PDF 预览在新标签打开。
            10. 非阻塞性观察（不影响业务结论）：Home 的 By month 表头存在未替换文案“{{count}} claims”；上传损坏的 corrupt.png 被接受为 Done 且预览 naturalWidth=0，未显示显式失败反馈；不支持格式 unsupported.exe 因 accept 过滤未进入列表，也无显式提示。控制台与 page errors 均为空。

            来源：verify-2/browser-acceptance/report.json · 原始状态：passed
